<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ سُلَيْمَانَ بْنِ يَسَارٍ، أَنَّ مُحَمَّدَ بْنَ الأَشْعَثِ، أَخْبَرَهُ أَنَّ عَمَّةً لَهُ يَهُودِيَّةً أَوْ نَصْرَانِيَّةً تُوُفِّيَتْ وَأَنَّ مُحَمَّدَ بْنَ الأَشْعَثِ ذَكَرَ ذَلِكَ لِعُمَرَ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ وَقَالَ لَهُ مَنْ يَرِثُهَا فَقَالَ لَهُ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ يَرِثُهَا أَهْلُ دِينِهَا ‏.‏ ثُمَّ أَتَى عُثْمَانَ بْنَ عَفَّانَ فَسَأَلَهُ عَنْ ذَلِكَ فَقَالَ لَهُ عُثْمَانُ أَتَرَانِي نَسِيتُ مَا قَالَ لَكَ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ يَرِثُهَا أَهْلُ دِينِهَا ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1085" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion); Uthman ibn Affan (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Yahya ibn Said from Sulayman ibn Yasar that Muhammad ibn al-Ashath told him that he had a christian or jewish paternal aunt who died. Muhammad ibn al-Ashath mentioned that to Umar ibn al-Khattab and said to him, &quot;Who inherits from her?&quot; Umar ibn al-Khattab said to him, &quot;The people of her deen inherit from her.&quot; Then he went to Uthman ibn Affan, and asked him about that. Uthman said to him, &quot;Do you think that I have forgotten what Umar ibn al-Khattab said to you? The people.of her deen inherit from her</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1085]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1085" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">मुहम्मद बिन अशअत की एक फुप्पी यहूदी थी या नस्रानी, मर गई। मुहम्मद बिन अशअत ने हज़रत उमर (रदी अल्लाहू अन्हु) से बयान किया और पूछा के इसका कौन वारिस होगा? उमर बिन खत्ताब (रदी अल्लाहू अन्हु) ने कहा उसके मज़हब वाले वारिस होंगे। फिर उस्मान बिन अफ़्फ़ान (रदी अल्लाहू अन्हु) जब ख़लीफ़ा हुए तो उनसे पूछा, उस्मान ने कहा क्या तू समझता है के उमर ने जो तुझ से कहा था उसको मैं भूल गया? वही उसके वारिस होंगे जो उसके मज़हब वाले हैं।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1085" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">محمد بن اشعث کی ایک پھوپھی یہودی تھی یا نصرانی مر گئی محمد بن اشعث نے حضرت عمر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ سے بیان کیا اور پوچھا کہ اس کا کون وارث ہوگا عمر بن خطاب رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ نے کہا اس کے مذہب والے وارث ہوں گے پھر عثمان رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ بن عفان جب خلیفہ ہوئے تو ان سے پوچھا عثمان نے کہا کیا تو سمجھتا ہے کہ عمر نے جو تجھ سے کہا تھا اس کو میں بھول گیا وہی اس کے وارث ہوں گے جو اس کے مذہب والے ہیں ۔</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Muhammad bin Ash&#x27;ath ki ek phuppi Yahudi thi ya Nasrani, mar gayi. Muhammad bin Ash&#x27;ath ne Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) se bayan kiya aur poochha ke iska kaun waris hoga? Umar bin Khattab (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha uske mazhab wale waris honge. Phir Usman bin Affan (Radi Allahu Anhu) jab khalifa hue toh unse poochha, Usman ne kaha kya tu samajhta hai ke Umar ne jo tujh se kaha tha usko main bhool gaya? Wohi uske waris honge jo uske mazhab wale hain.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1085" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ إِسْمَاعِيلَ بْنِ أَبِي حَكِيمٍ، أَنَّ نَصْرَانِيًّا، أَعْتَقَهُ عُمَرُ بْنُ عَبْدِ الْعَزِيزِ هَلَكَ - قَالَ إِسْمَاعِيلُ - فَأَمَرَنِي عُمَرُ بْنُ عَبْدِ الْعَزِيزِ أَنْ أَجْعَلَ مَالَهُ فِي بَيْتِ الْمَالِ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1086" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: historical report. Attribution: Umar ibn Abd al-Aziz (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Yahya ibn Said from Ismail ibn Abi Hakim that Umar ibn Abd al-Aziz freed a christian who then died. Ismail said, &#x27;&#x27;Umar ibn Abd al-Aziz ordered me to put his property in the bayt al-mal</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1086]</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya ne mujh se Malik se, unhone Yahya bin Saeed se, unhone Ismail bin Abi Hakim se riwayat kiya ke Hazrat Umar bin Abdul Aziz ne ek Isai ko azad kiya jis ka baad mein inteqal ho gaya. Ismail ne kaha ke Umar bin Abdul Aziz ne mujhe hukm diya ke us ka maal Bait-ul-Maal mein daal do.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1086" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

## Narration 1078

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1087)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 27</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1087]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1087" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1087</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1087 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ الثِّقَةِ، عِنْدَهُ أَنَّهُ سَمِعَ سَعِيدَ بْنَ الْمُسَيَّبِ، يَقُولُ أَبَى عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ أَنْ يُوَرِّثَ، أَحَدًا مِنَ الأَعَاجِمِ إِلاَّ أَحَدًا وُلِدَ فِي الْعَرَبِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَإِنْ جَاءَتِ امْرَأَةٌ حَامِلٌ مِنْ أَرْضِ الْعَدُوِّ فَوَضَعَتْهُ فِي أَرْضِ الْعَرَبِ فَهُوَ وَلَدُهَا يَرِثُهَا إِنْ مَاتَتْ وَتَرِثُهُ إِنْ مَاتَ مِيرَاثَهَا فِي كِتَابِ اللَّهِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ الأَمْرُ الْمُجْتَمَعُ عَلَيْهِ عِنْدَنَا وَالسُّنَّةُ الَّتِي لاَ اخْتِلاَفَ فِيهَا وَالَّذِي أَدْرَكْتُ عَلَيْهِ أَهْلَ الْعِلْمِ بِبَلَدِنَا أَنَّهُ لاَ يَرِثُ الْمُسْلِمُ الْكَافِرَ بِقَرَابَةٍ وَلاَ وَلاَءٍ وَلاَ رَحِمٍ وَلاَ يَحْجُبُ أَحَدًا عَنْ مِيرَاثِهِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَكَذَلِكَ كُلُّ مَنْ لاَ يَرِثُ إِذَا لَمْ يَكُنْ دُونَهُ وَارِثٌ فَإِنَّهُ لاَ يَحْجُبُ أَحَدًا عَنْ مِيرَاثِهِ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1087" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from a reliable source of his who had heard Said ibn al-Musayyab say, &#x27;&#x27;Umar ibn al-Khattab refused to let anyone inherit from the non-arabs except for one who was born among the arabs.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1087]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1087" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1087</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1087 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;If a pregnant woman comes from the land of the enemy and gives birth in arab land so that he is her (an arab) child, he inherits from her if she dies, and she inherits from him if he dies, by the Book of Allah.&quot; Malik said, &quot;The generally agreed on way of doing things among us and the sunna in which there is no dispute, and what I saw the people of knowledge in our city doing, is that a Muslim does not inherit from a kafir by kinship, clientage (wala&#x27;), or maternal relationship, nor does he (the Muslim) overshadow any (of the kafirs) from his inheritance. Malik said, &quot;Similarly, someone who forgoes his inheritance when he is the chief heir does not overshadow anyone from his inheritance</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1087" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1087]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1087" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1087</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1087 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">इस्माईल बिन आबि हकीम से रिवायत है के उमर बिन अब्दुल अज़ीज़ का एक घुलम नसरानी था मर गया तोह उमर बिन अब्दुल अज़ीज़ ने मुझ से कहा कि इसका माल बैत-उल-माल में दाखिल कर दो. सईद बिन मुसय्यब कहते थे के उमर बिन खत्ताब (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने इंकार किया गैर मुल्क के लोगों की मीरास दिलाने का अपने मुल्क वालों की मगर जो अरब में पैदा हुआ हो. कहा मालिक ने अगर एक औरत हामिला कुफ्फार के मुल्क में से आ कर अरब में रहे और वहां बचा जाने तोह वह अपने लड़के की वारिस होगी और लड़का उसका वारिस होगा. कहा मालिक ने हमारे नज़दीक यह हुक्म इज्म&#x27;आई है और इस में कुछ इख्तिलाफ नहीं है के मुसलमान काफ़िर का किसी रिश्ते की वजह से वारिस नहीं हो सकता ख़्वाह वह रिश्ता नाने का हो या वाला का या क़राबत का और न किसी को उसकी विरासत से महरूम कर सकता है. कहा मालिक ने इसी तरह जो शख्स मीरास न पाये वह दूसरे को मेहरूम नहीं कर सकता.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1087" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">اسمعیل بن ابی حکیم سے روایت ہے کہ عمر بن عبدالعزیز کا ایک غلام نصرانی تھا اس کو انہوں نے آزاد کر دیا وہ مر گیا تو عمر بن عبدالعزیز نے مجھ سے کہا کہ اس کا مال بیت المال میں داخل کر دو ۔ سعید بن مسیب کہتے تھے کہ عمر بن خطاب نے انکار کیا غیر ملک کے لوگوں کی میراث دلانے کا اپنے ملک والوں کی مگر جو عرب میں پیدا ہوا ہو۔ کہا مالک نے اگر ایک عورت حاملہ کفار کے ملک میں سے آ کر عرب میں رہے اور وہاں (بچہ ) جنے تو وہ اپنے لڑکے کی وارث ہوگی اور لڑکا اس کا وارث ہو گا۔ کہا مالک نے ہمارے نزدیک یہ حکم اجماعی ہے اور اس میں کچھ اختلاف نہیں ہے کہ مسلمان کافر کا کسی رشتہ کی وجہ سے وارث نہیں ہوسکتا خواہ وہ رشتہ نانے کا ہو یا ولاء کا یا قرابت کا اور نہ کسی کو اس کی وارثت سے محروم کرسکتا ہے۔ کہا مالک نے اسی طرح جو شخص میراث نہ پائے وہ دوسرے کو محروم نہیں کرسکتا۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1087" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1087]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1087" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Roman Urdu · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1087</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1087 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Ismaeel bin Abi Hakeem se riwayat hai ke Umar bin Abdul Aziz ka ek ghulam Nasrani tha, usko unhone azad kar diya. Woh mar gaya toh Umar bin Abdul Aziz ne mujh se kaha ke iska maal Bait-ul-Maal mein dakhil kar do. Saeed bin Musayyib kehte the ke Umar bin Khattab (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne inkaar kiya ghair mulk ke logon ki meeras dilane ka apne mulk walon ki, magar jo Arab mein paida hua ho. Kaha Malik ne agar ek aurat hamila kuffar ke mulk mein se aa kar Arab mein rahe aur wahan bacha jane toh woh apne ladke ki waris hogi aur ladka uska waris hoga. Kaha Malik ne hamare nazdeek yeh hukm ijm&#x27;ai hai aur is mein kuch ikhtilaf nahi hai ke musalman kafir ka kisi rishta ki wajah se waris nahi ho sakta khwah woh rishta nane ka ho ya wala ka ya qarabat ka aur na kisi ko uski wirasat se mehroom kar sakta hai. Kaha Malik ne isi tarah jo shakhs meeras na paye woh dusre ko mehroom nahi kar sakta.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1087" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ رَبِيعَةَ بْنِ أَبِي عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، عَنْ غَيْرِ، وَاحِدٍ، مِنْ عُلَمَائِهِمْ ‏.‏ أَنَّهُ لَمْ يَتَوَارَثْ مَنْ قُتِلَ يَوْمَ الْجَمَلِ وَيَوْمَ صِفِّينَ وَيَوْمَ الْحَرَّةِ ثُمَّ كَانَ يَوْمَ قُدَيْدٍ فَلَمْ يُوَرَّثْ أَحَدٌ مِنْهُمْ مِنْ صَاحِبِهِ شَيْئًا إِلاَّ مَنْ عُلِمَ أَنَّهُ قُتِلَ قَبْلَ صَاحِبِهِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَذَلِكَ الأَمْرُ الَّذِي لاَ اخْتِلاَفَ فِيهِ وَلاَ شَكَّ عِنْدَ أَحَدٍ مِنْ أَهْلِ الْعِلْمِ بِبَلَدِنَا وَكَذَلِكَ الْعَمَلُ فِي كُلِّ مُتَوَارِثَيْنِ هَلَكَا بِغَرَقٍ أَوْ قَتْلٍ أَوْ غَيْرِ ذَلِكَ مِنَ الْمَوْتِ إِذَا لَمْ يُعْلَمْ أَيُّهُمَا مَاتَ قَبْلَ صَاحِبِهِ لَمْ يَرِثْ أَحَدٌ مِنْهُمَا مِنْ صَاحِبِهِ شَيْئًا وَكَانَ مِيرَاثُهُمَا لِمَنْ بَقِيَ مِنْ وَرَثَتِهِمَا يَرِثُ كُلَّ وَاحِدٍ مِنْهُمَا وَرَثَتُهُ مِنَ الأَحْيَاءِ ‏.‏ وَقَالَ مَالِكٌ لاَ يَنْبَغِي أَنْ يَرِثَ أَحَدٌ أَحَدًا بِالشَّكِّ وَلاَ يَرِثُ أَحَدٌ أَحَدًا إِلاَّ بِالْيَقِينِ مِنَ الْعِلْمِ وَالشُّهَدَاءِ وَذَلِكَ أَنَّ الرَّجُلَ يَهْلِكُ هُوَ وَمَوْلاَهُ الَّذِي أَعْتَقَهُ أَبُوهُ فَيَقُولُ بَنُو الرَّجُلِ الْعَرَبِيِّ قَدْ وَرِثَهُ أَبُونَا فَلَيْسَ ذَلِكَ لَهُمْ أَنْ يَرِثُوهُ بِغَيْرِ عِلْمٍ وَلاَ شَهَادَةٍ إِنَّهُ مَاتَ قَبْلَهُ وَإِنَّمَا يَرِثُهُ أَوْلَى النَّاسِ بِهِ مِنَ الأَحْيَاءِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَمِنْ ذَلِكَ أَيْضًا الأَخَوَانِ لِلأَبِ وَالأُمِّ يَمُوتَانِ وَلأَحَدِهِمَا وَلَدٌ وَالآخَرُ لاَ وَلَدَ لَهُ وَلَهُمَا أَخٌ لأَبِيهِمَا فَلاَ يُعْلَمُ أَيُّهُمَا مَاتَ قَبْلَ صَاحِبِهِ فَمِيرَاثُ الَّذِي لاَ وَلَدَ لَهُ لأَخِيهِ لأَبِيهِ وَلَيْسَ لِبَنِي أَخِيهِ لأَبِيهِ وَأُمِّهِ شَىْءٌ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَمِنْ ذَلِكَ أَيْضًا أَنْ تَهْلَكَ الْعَمَّةُ وَابْنُ أَخِيهَا أَوِ ابْنَةُ الأَخِ وَعَمُّهَا فَلاَ يُعْلَمُ أَيُّهُمَا مَاتَ قَبْلُ فَإِنْ لَمْ يُعْلَمْ أَيُّهُمَا مَاتَ قَبْلُ لَمْ يَرِثِ الْعَمُّ مِنِ ابْنَةِ أَخِيهِ شَيْئًا وَلاَ يَرِثُ ابْنُ الأَخِ مِنْ عَمَّتِهِ شَيْئًا ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1088" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: historical report. Attribution: unnamed in the supplied field (unresolved). The source describes inheritance practice following four named historical battles. The unnamed informants remain unresolved; subsequent compiler hypotheticals stay context.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Rabia ibn Abi Abd ar-Rahman from more than one of the people of knowledge of that time, that those who were killed on the Day of the Camel, the Day of Siffin, the Day of al-Harra, and the Day of Qudayd did not inherit from each other. None of them inherited anything from his companion unless it was known that he had been killed before his companion.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1088]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1088" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1088</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1088 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;That is the way of doing things about which there is no dispute, and which none of the people of knowledge in our city doubt. The procedure with two mutual heirs who are drowned, or killed in another way, when it is not known which of them died first is the same - neither of them inherits anything from his companion. Their inheritance goes to whoever remains of their heirs. They are inherited from by the living.&quot; Malik said, &quot;No one should inherit from anyone else when there is doubt, and one should only inherit from the other when there is certainty of knowledge and witnesses. That is because a man and his mawla whom his father has freed might die at the same time. The sons of the free man could say, &#x27;Our father inherited from the mawla.&#x27; They should not inherit from the mawla without knowledge or testimony that he died first. The living people most entitled to his wala&#x27; inherit from him.&quot; Malik said, &quot;Another example is two full brothers who die. One of them has children and the other does not. They have a half-brother by their father. It is not known which of them died first, so the inheritance of the childless one goes to his half-brother by the father. The children of the full-brother get nothing.&quot; Malik said, &quot;Another example is when a paternal aunt and the son of her brother die, or else the daughter of the brother and her paternal uncle. It is not known which of them died first. The paternal uncle does not inherit anything from the daughter of his brother, and the son of the brother does not inherit anything from his paternal aunt</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1088" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1088 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">राबिया बिन अबी अब्दुल रहमान और बहुत से उलेमा से रिवायत है के जितने लोग क़त्ल हुए जंग-ए-जमल और जंग-ए-सिफ़्फ़ीन और यौम-उल-हर्रा में और जो यौम-उल-क़दीद में मारे गए, वो आपस में एक दूसरे के वारिस नहीं हुए मगर जिस शख़्स का हाल मालूम हो गया के वो अपने वारिस से पहले मारा गया। कहा मालिक ने यही हुक्म है अगर कोई आदमी डूब जाए या मकान से गिर कर मारे जाएं या क़त्ल किए जाएं जब मालूम न हो सके के पहले कौन मरा और बाद में कौन मरा तो आपस में एक दूसरे के वारिस न होंगे बल्कि हर एक का तरका उसके वारिसों को जो ज़िंदा हों पहुंचेगा। कहा मालिक ने कोई किसी का वारिस शक से न होगा बल्कि इल्म-ओ-यक़ीन से वारिस होगा, मसलन एक शख़्स मर जाए और उसके बाप का मौला (ग़ुलाम आज़ाद किया हुआ) मर जाए, अब उसके बेटे ये कहें इस मौला का वारिस हमारा बाप था तो ये नहीं हो सकता जब तक के इल्म-ओ-यक़ीन या गवाहों से ये साबित न हो के पहले मौला मरा था। उस वक़्त तक मौला के वारिस जो ज़िंदा हों इस तरका को पाएंगे। कहा मालिक ने इसी तरह अगर सगे दो भाई मर जाएं एक की औलाद हो और दूसरा ला-औलाद हो, उन दोनों का एक सौतेला भाई भी हो, फिर मालूम न हो सके के पहले कौन सा भाई मरा है तो जो भाई ला-औलाद मरा है उसका तरका उसके सौतेले भाई को मिलेगा, उसके भतीजों को न मिलेगा।</p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">ربیعہ بن ابی عبدالرحمن اور بہت سے علماء سے روایت ہے کہ جتنے لوگ قتل ہوئے جنگ جمل اور جنگ صفین اور یوم الحرہ میں اور جو یوم القدید میں مارے گئے وہ آپس میں ایک دوسرے کے وارث نہیں ہوئے مگر جس شخص کا حال معلوم ہو گیا کہ وہ اپنے وارث سے پہلے مارا گیا ۔ کہا مالک نے یہی حکم ہے اگر کوئی آدمی ڈوب جائے یا مکان سے گر کر مارے جائیں یا قتل کئے جائیں جب معلوم نہ ہوسکے کہ پہلے کون مرا اور بعد میں کون مرا تو آپس میں ایک دوسرے کے وارث نہ ہوں گے بلکہ ہر ایک کا ترکہ اس کے وارثوں کو جو زندہ ہوں پہنچے گا۔ کہا مالک نے کوئی کسی کا وارث شک سے نہ ہوگا بلکہ علم و یقین سے وارث ہوگا مثلا ایک شخص مرجائے اور اس کے باپ کا مولیٰ (غلام آزاد کیا ہوا) مرجائے اب اس کے بیٹے یہ کہیں اس مولیٰ کا وارث ہمارا باپ تھا تو یہ نہیں ہوسکتا جب تک کہ علم ویقین یا گواہوں سے یہ ثابت نہ ہو کہ پہلے مولیٰ مرا تھا ۔ اس وقت تک مولیٰ کے وارث جو زندہ ہوں اس ترکہ کو پائیں گے ۔ کہا مالک نے اسی طرح اگر سگے دو بھائی مرجائیں ایک کی اولاد ہو اور دوسرا لاولد ہو ان دونوں کا ایک سوتیلا بھائی بھی ہو پھر معلوم نہ ہوسکے کہ پہلے کون سا بھائی مرا ہے تو جو بھائی لاولد مرا ہے اس کا ترکہ اس کے سوتیلے بھائی کو ملے گا اس کے بھتیجوں کو نہ ملے گا۔</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Rabia bin Abi Abdul Rahman aur bahut se ulama se riwayat hai ke jitne log qatl hue Jang-e-Jamal aur Jang-e-Siffeen aur Youm-ul-Harra mein aur jo Youm-ul-Qadeed mein mare gaye, woh aapas mein ek dusre ke waris nahi hue magar jis shakhs ka haal maloom ho gaya ke woh apne waris se pehle mara gaya. Kaha Malik ne yahi hukm hai agar koi aadmi doob jaye ya makan se gir kar mare jayein ya qatl kiye jayein jab maloom na ho sake ke pehle kaun mara aur baad mein kaun mara toh aapas mein ek dusre ke waris na honge balki har ek ka tarka uske warison ko jo zinda hon pahunchega. Kaha Malik ne koi kisi ka waris shak se na hoga balki ilm-o-yaqeen se waris hoga, maslan ek shakhs mar jaye aur uske baap ka maula (ghulam azad kiya hua) mar jaye, ab uske bete yeh kahein is maula ka waris hamara baap tha toh yeh nahi ho sakta jab tak ke ilm-o-yaqeen ya gawahon se yeh sabit na ho ke pehle maula mara tha. Us waqt tak maula ke waris jo zinda hon is tarka ko payenge. Kaha Malik ne isi tarah agar sage do bhai mar jayein ek ki aulad ho aur dusra la-aulad ho, un dono ka ek sautela bhai bhi ho, phir maloom na ho sake ke pehle kaun sa bhai mara hai toh jo bhai la-aulad mara hai uska tarka uske sautele bhai ko milega, uske bhatijon ko na milega.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1088" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ عُرْوَةَ بْنَ الزُّبَيْرِ، كَانَ يَقُولُ فِي وَلَدِ الْمُلاَعَنَةِ وَوَلَدِ الزِّنَا إِنَّهُ إِذَا مَاتَ وَرِثَتْهُ أُمُّهُ حَقَّهَا فِي كِتَابِ اللَّهِ عَزَّ وَجَلَّ وَإِخْوَتُهُ لأُمِّهِ حُقُوقَهُمْ وَيَرِثُ الْبَقِيَّةَ مَوَالِي أُمِّهِ إِنْ كَانَتْ مَوْلاَةً وَإِنْ كَانَتْ عَرَبِيَّةً وَرِثَتْ حَقَّهَا وَوَرِثَ إِخْوَتُهُ لأُمِّهِ حُقُوقَهُمْ وَكَانَ مَا بَقِيَ لِلْمُسْلِمِينَ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَبَلَغَنِي عَنْ سُلَيْمَانَ بْنِ يَسَارٍ، مِثْلُ ذَلِكَ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَعَلَى ذَلِكَ أَدْرَكْتُ أَهْلَ الْعِلْمِ بِبَلَدِنَا ‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1089" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">This source entry preserves two separately attributed reports in their original order. They remain one provider entry, with their distinct narrator introductions and source references. Report type: successor report. Attribution: Urwa ibn az-Zubayr (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words. Report type: abbreviated transmission. Attribution: Sulayman ibn Yasar (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 1</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that Urwa ibn az-Zubayr said about the child of lian and the child of fornication, that if they died, the mother inherited her right from them according to the Book of Allah, the Mighty, the Majestic! The siblings by the mother had their rights. The rest was inherited by the former masters of the mother if she was a freed slave. If she was a free woman by origin, she inherited her due and the siblings by the mother inherited their due, and the rest went to the Muslims.</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 2</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik said, &quot;I heard the same as that from Sulayman ibn Yasar.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1089]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1089" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>

<p dir="auto">उरवा बिन ज़ुबैर कहते थे के ल&#x27;आन वाली औरत का लड़का या ज़िना का लड़का जब मर जाये तोह उसकी माँ किताबुल्लाह के मुवाफ़िक़ अपना हिस्सा लेगी और जो उसके माद्रि भाई हैं वह अपना हिस्सा लेंगे हुई हो और अगर अरबिया हो तोह बाद माँ और भाई बहनों के हिस्से के जो बचेगा वह मुसलमानों का हक़ होगा. कहा मालिक ने सुलईमान बिन यसर से भी मुझे ऐसा ही पहुंचा और हमारे शहर के अहल-इ-इल्म की यही राय है.</p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">عروہ بن زبیر کہتے تھے کہ لعان والی عورت کا لڑکا یا زنا کا لڑکا جب مر جائے تو اس کی ماں کتاب اللہ کے موافق اپنا حصہ لے گی اور جو اس کے مادری بھائی ہیں وہ اپنا حصہ لیں گے باقی اس کی ماں کے موالی کو ملے گا اگر وہ آزاد کی ہوئی ہو اور اگر عربیہ ہو تو بعد ماں اور بھائی بہنوں کے حصے کے جو بچے گا وہ مسلمانوں کا حق ہوگا ۔ کہا مالک نے سلیمان بن یسار سے بھی مجھے ایسا ہی پہنچا اور ہمارے شہر کے اہل علم کی یہی رائے ہے۔</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Urwa bin Zubair kehte the ke La&#x27;an wali aurat ka ladka ya zina ka ladka jab mar jaye toh uski maa Kitabullah ke muwafiq apna hissa legi aur jo uske maadri bhai hain woh apna hissa lenge, baqi uski maa ke mawali ko milega agar woh azad ki hui ho aur agar Arabiya ho toh baad maa aur bhai behnon ke hisse ke jo bachega woh musalmanon ka haq hoga. Kaha Malik ne Sulaiman bin Yasar se bhi mujhe aisa hi pahuncha aur hamare shehar ke ahl-e-ilm ki yahi rai hai.</p>

## Narration 1081

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1091)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 28</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ يَحْيَى بْنِ حَبَّانَ، عَنِ الأَعْرَجِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ لاَ يَخْطُبُ أَحَدُكُمْ عَلَى خِطْبَةِ أَخِيهِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1091]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1091" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Muhammad ibn Yahya ibn Habban from al-Araj from Abu Hurayra that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Do not ask for a woman in marriage when another muslim has already done so</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1091]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1091" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू हुरैरा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: तुम में से कोई शख्स अपने मुसलमान भाई के पैग़ाम-इ-निकाह पर अपना पैग़ाम न भेजे.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1091]</p>

## Narration 1082

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1092)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 28</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ لاَ يَخْطُبُ أَحَدُكُمْ عَلَى خِطْبَةِ أَخِيهِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1092]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1092" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1092</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1092 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi from Abdullah ibn Umar that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Do not ask for a woman in marriage when another muslim has already done so.&quot; Malik said, &quot;The explanation of the statement of the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, according to what we think - and Allah knows best - is that &#x27;Do not ask for a woman in marriage when another muslim has already done so&#x27; means that when a man has asked for a woman in marriage, and she has inclined to him and they have agreed on a bride-price, which she has suggested and with which they are mutually satisfied, it is forbidden for another man to ask for that woman in marriage. It does not mean that when a man has asked for a woman in marriage, and his suit does not agree with her and she does not incline to him that no one else can ask for her in marriage. That is a door to misery for people</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1092]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1092" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर (रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: तुम में से कोई शख़्स अपने मुसलमान भाई के पैग़ाम-ए-निकाह पर अपना पैग़ाम न भेजे। इमाम मालिक (रहमतुल्लाह अलैह) ने कहा इस हदीस का मतलब ये है के जब एक शख़्स की निस्बत किसी औरत से ठहर जाए और औरत का दिल उस मर्द की तरफ़ मायल हो जाए और मेहर मुक़र्रर हो जाए, अब उस औरत को दूसरा शख़्स पैग़ाम न दे। और अगर किसी शख़्स ने पैग़ाम दिया हो लेकिन अभी बात पक्की न हुई हो, तो दूसरे का पैग़ाम देना दुरुस्त नहीं।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1092]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1092" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن عمر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا نہ پیغام بھیجے نکاح کا کوئی تم میں سے اپنے بھائی مسلمان کے پیغام پر ۔ کہا مالک نے اس حدیث کا مطلب یہ ہے کہ جب ایک شخص کی نسبت کسی عور رت سے ٹھہر جائے اور عورت کا دل کسی مرد کی طرف مائل ہو جائے اور مہر ٹھہر جائے اب پھر اس عورت کو دوسرا شخص پیام نہ دے اور غرض نہیں کہ کسی شخص نے ایک عورت کو پیام دیا ہو اور اس کا پیام ٹھہرا نہ ہو تو دوسرے کو پیام درست نہیں ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1092]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1092" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Tum mein se koi shakhs apne musalman bhai ke paigham-e-nikah par apna paigham na bheje. Imam Malik (Rahmatullah Alaih) ne kaha is hadith ka matlab yeh hai ke jab ek shakhs ki nisbat kisi aurat se thehar jaye aur aurat ka dil us mard ki taraf mayal ho jaye aur mehar muqarrar ho jaye, ab us aurat ko dusra shakhs paigham na de. Aur agar kisi shakhs ne paigham diya ho lekin abhi baat pakki na hui ho, to dusre ka paigham dena durust nahi.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1092]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1092" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1083

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1093)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 28</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1093]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1093" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1093</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1093 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ الْقَاسِمِ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، أَنَّهُ كَانَ يَقُولُ فِي قَوْلِ اللَّهِ تَبَارَكَ وَتَعَالَى ‏{‏وَلاَ جُنَاحَ عَلَيْكُمْ فِيمَا عَرَّضْتُمْ بِهِ مِنْ خِطْبَةِ النِّسَاءِ أَوْ أَكْنَنْتُمْ فِي أَنْفُسِكُمْ عَلِمَ اللَّهُ أَنَّكُمْ سَتَذْكُرُونَهُنَّ وَلَكِنْ لاَ تُوَاعِدُوهُنَّ سِرًّا إِلاَّ أَنْ تَقُولُوا قَوْلاً مَعْرُوفًا‏}‏ أَنْ يَقُولَ الرَّجُلُ لِلْمَرْأَةِ وَهِيَ فِي عِدَّتِهَا مِنْ وَفَاةِ زَوْجِهَا إِنَّكِ عَلَىَّ لَكَرِيمَةٌ وَإِنِّي فِيكِ لَرَاغِبٌ وَإِنَّ اللَّهَ لَسَائِقٌ إِلَيْكِ خَيْرًا وَرِزْقًا وَنَحْوَ هَذَا مِنَ الْقَوْلِ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1093" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: attributed saying. Attribution: Al-Qasim, named as Abd ar-Rahman ibn al-Qasims father in the transmitted chain. The full field is a specifically transmitted early-authority explanation of the verse about proposals during a widows waiting period. Preserve it as al-Qasims attributed saying, with its quoted verse and examples, without calling it Prophetic or Maliks own ruling.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abd ar-Rahman ibn al-Qasim that his father said about the word of Allah, the Blessed, the Exalted, &quot;There is no fault in you about the proposal you offer to women, or hide in yourselves. Allah knows that you will be mindful of them; but do not make troth with them secretly without honourable words,&quot; (Sura 2 ayat 235) that it referred to a man saying to a woman while she was still in her idda after the death of her husband, &quot;You are dear to me, and I desire you, and Allah brings provision and blessing to you,&quot; and words such as these</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1093]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1093" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">क़ासिम बिन मुहम्मद इस आयत की तफ़सीर में कहते थे: (वला जुनाहा अलैकुम फ़ीमा अर्रदतुम बिही मिन ख़ितबतिन्निसा...) यानी तुम पर कोई गुनाह नहीं के तुम इशारा-कुना-यां बात करो किसी औरत से जब वो इद्दत में हो। ताअरीज़ (इशारा) उसे कहते हैं के मर्द औरत से कहे, &quot;मैं तुझे पसंद करता हूँ&quot; या &quot;मैं तुझ से रग़बत रखता हूँ&quot; या &quot;अल्लाह तुझे बेहतरी और रोज़ी देने वाला है&quot; या इसी तरह की कोई और बात कहे।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1093" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">قاسم بن محمد کہتے تھے اس آیت کی تفسیر میں (وَلَا جُنَاحَ عَلَيْكُمْ فِ يْمَا عَرَّضْتُمْ بِه مِنْ خِطْبَةِ النِّسَا ءِ اَوْ اَكْنَنْتُمْ فِيْ اَنْفُسِكُمْ عَلِمَ اللّٰهُ اَنَّكُمْ سَتَذْكُرُوْنَهُنَّ وَلٰكِنْ لَّا تُوَاعِدُوْھُنَّ سِرًّا اِلَّا اَنْ تَقُوْلُوْا قَوْلًا مَّعْرُوْفًا ڛ وَلَا تَعْزِمُوْا عُقْدَةَ النِّكَاحِ حَتّٰي يَبْلُغَ الْكِتٰبُ اَجَلَه وَاعْلَمُوْ ا اَنَّ اللّٰهَ يَعْلَمُ مَا فِىْ اَنْفُسِكُمْ فَاحْذَرُوْهُ وَاعْلَمُوْ ا اَنَّ اللّٰهَ غَفُوْرٌ حَلِيْمٌ ) 2۔ البقرۃ : 235) یعنی گناہ نہیں ہے تم پر تعریض کرنا کسی عورت سے جب وہ عدت میں ہو۔ تعریض اس کو کہتے ہیں کہ مرد عورت سے کہلا بھیجے تو مجھے پسند ہے یا میں تجھ سے رغبت کرتا ہوں یا اللہ تجھ کو بہتری اور روزی پہنچانے والا ہے یا ایسی کوئی بات کہے ۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1093" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Qasim bin Muhammad is aayat ki tafseer mein kehte the: (Wala junaha alaikum fima arradtum bihi min khitbatinnisa...) yani tum par koi gunah nahi ke tum ishara-kuna-ya baat karo kisi aurat se jab woh iddat mein ho. Ta&#x27;areez (ishara) usay kehte hain ke mard aurat se kahe, &quot;Main tujhe pasand karta hoon&quot; ya &quot;Main tujh se raghbat rakhta hoon&quot; ya &quot;Allah tujhe behtari aur rozi dene wala hai&quot; ya isi tarah ki koi aur baat kahe.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1093" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

## Narration 1084

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1094)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 28</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ الْفَضْلِ، عَنْ نَافِعِ بْنِ جُبَيْرِ بْنِ مُطْعِمٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَبَّاسٍ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ الأَيِّمُ أَحَقُّ بِنَفْسِهَا مِنْ وَلِيِّهَا وَالْبِكْرُ تُسْتَأْذَنُ فِي نَفْسِهَا وَإِذْنُهَا صُمَاتُهَا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1094]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1094" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1094</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1094 ]</p>

</details>

<details>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Abdullah ibn al-Fadl from Nafi ibn Jubayr ibn Mutim from Abdullah ibn Abbas that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;A woman who has been previously married is more entitled to her person than her guardian, and a virgin must be asked for her consent for herself, and her consent is her silence</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1094]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1094" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1094 ]</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">इमाम मालिक ने अब्दुल्लाह बिन फ़ज़ल से, उन्होंने नाफ़े बिन जुबैर से, उन्होंने अब्दुल्लाह बिन अब्बास से रिवायत की के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: बेवा औरत अपने वली से ज़्यादा अपनी ज़ात की हक़दार है और कुंवारी लड़की से उस की इजाज़त ली जाए और उस की ख़ामोशी ही उस की इजाज़त है।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1094]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1094" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">امام مالک نے عبداللہ بن فضل سے، انہوں نے نافع بن جبیر سے، انہوں نے عبداللہ بن عباس سے روایت کی کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: بیوہ عورت اپنے ولی سے زیادہ اپنی ذات کی حقدار ہے اور کنواری لڑکی سے اس کی اجازت لی جائے اور اس کی خاموشی ہی اس کی اجازت ہے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1094]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1094" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Imam Malik ne Abdullah bin Fazl se, unhone Nafi bin Jubayr se, unhone Abdullah bin Abbas se riwayat ki ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Bewah aurat apne wali se zyada apni zaat ki haqdar hai aur kunwari ladki se us ki ijazat li jaye aur us ki khamoshi hi us ki ijazat hai.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1094]</p>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ الْمُسَيَّبِ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ قَالَ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ لاَ تُنْكَحُ الْمَرْأَةُ إِلاَّ بِإِذْنِ وَلِيِّهَا أَوْ ذِي الرَّأْىِ مِنْ أَهْلِهَا أَوِ السُّلْطَانِ ‏.‏</p>

<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that Said ibn al-Musayyab had said that Umar ibn al-Khattab said, &quot;A woman is only married with the consent of her guardian, someone of her family with sound judgement or the Sultan</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1095]</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya ne mujh se Imam Malik se riwayat kiya ke unhone suna ke Saeed bin Musayyab ne kaha ke Hazrat Umar bin Khattab (Razi Allah-o-Anhu) ne farmaya: Aurat ka nikah sirf us ke wali ki ijazat se hota hai, yani us ke khandan ka samajhdar shakhs ya phir Sultan.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1095" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 28</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ الْقَاسِمَ بْنَ مُحَمَّدٍ، وَسَالِمَ بْنَ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ، كَانَا يُنْكِحَانِ بَنَاتِهِمَا الأَبْكَارَ وَلاَ يَسْتَأْمِرَانِهِنَّ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَذَلِكَ الأَمْرُ عِنْدَنَا فِي نِكَاحِ الأَبْكَارِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَلَيْسَ لِلْبِكْرِ جَوَازٌ فِي مَالِهَا حَتَّى تَدْخُلَ بَيْتَهَا وَيُعْرَفَ مِنْ حَالِهَا ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1096" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: al-Qasim ibn Muhammad (successor); Salim ibn Abdullah (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that al-Qasim ibn Muhammad and Salim ibn Abdullah were marrying off their daughters and they did not consult them.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1096]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1096" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1096 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;That is what is done among us about the marriage of virgins.&quot; Malik said, &quot;A virgin has no right to her property until she enters her house and her state (competence, maturity etc.) is known for sure</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1096" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">इब्न अब्बास (रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: बेवा (थय्यिबह) अपने नफ़्स पर अपने वली से ज़्यादा हक़दार है, और कुंवारी (बाकिराह) से इजाज़त ली जाएगी और उसकी इजाज़त उसका ख़ामोश रहना है। सईद बिन मुसय्यिब से रिवायत है के हज़रत उमर (रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने फ़रमाया: औरत का निकाह उसके वली की इजाज़त के बग़ैर न किया जाए, या उसके ख़ानदान में से कोई अक़्लमंद शख़्स हो तो उसकी इजाज़त से, या बादशाह की इजाज़त से। क़ासिम बिन मुहम्मद और सालिम बिन अब्दुल्लाह अपनी बेटियों का निकाह करते थे और उन से नहीं पूछते थे। क़ासिम, सालिम और सुलैमान बिन यासर कहते थे के अगर कुंवारी औरत का निकाह उसकी इजाज़त के बग़ैर कर दिया जाए तो निकाह लाज़िम हो जाता है।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1096" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">ابن عباس رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہما سے روایت ہے کہ آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا ثیبہ زیادہ حقدار ہے اپنے نفس پر ولی سے اور باکرہ سے اذن لیا جائے گا اور اذن اس کا سکوت ہے ۔ سعید بن مسیب سے روایت ہے کہ حضرت عمر نے فرمایا عورت کا نکاح نہ کیا جائے مگر اس کے ولی کے اذن سے یا اس کے کنبے میں یا جو شخص عقلمند ہو اس کے اذن سے یا بادشاہ کے اذن سے ۔ قاسم بن محمد اور سالم بن عبداللہ اپنی بیٹیوں کا نکاح کرتے تھے اور ان سے نہیں پوچھتے تھے ۔ قاسم بن محمد اور سالم بن عبداللہ اور سلیمان بن یسار کہتے تھے اگر باکرہ عورت کا نکاح اس کے اذن کے بغیر کر دے تو نکاح اس کا لازم ہو جاتا ہے ۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1096" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1096" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1096 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Ibn Abbas (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Bewa (thayyibah) apne nafs par apne wali se zyada haqdar hai, aur kunwari (bakirah) se ijazat li jayegi aur uski ijazat uska khamosh rehna hai. Saeed bin Musayyib se riwayat hai ke Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne farmaya: Aurat ka nikah uske wali ki ijazat ke baghair na kiya jaye, ya uske khandan mein se koi aqalmand shakhs ho to uski ijazat se, ya badshah ki ijazat se. Qasim bin Muhammad aur Salim bin Abdullah apni betiyon ka nikah karte the aur un se nahi puchte the. Qasim, Salim aur Sulaiman bin Yasar kehte the ke agar kunwari aurat ka nikah uski ijazat ke baghair kar diya jaye to nikah lazim ho jata hai.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1096" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">यहया ने मुझ से इमाम मालिक से रिवायत किया के उन्होंने सुना के कासिम बिन मुहम्मद, सालिम बिन अब्दुल्लाह और सुलयमान बिन यासार ने उस कुंवारी लड़की के बारे में जिस का निकाह उस के बाप ने उस की इजाज़त के बग़ैर कर दिया, कहा के ये उस पर लाज़िम है।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1097" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1088

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1098)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 28</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ أَبِي حَازِمِ بْنِ دِينَارٍ، عَنْ سَهْلِ بْنِ سَعْدٍ السَّاعِدِيِّ، ‏.‏ أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم جَاءَتْهُ امْرَأَةٌ فَقَالَتْ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ إِنِّي قَدْ وَهَبْتُ نَفْسِي لَكَ فَقَامَتْ قِيَامًا طَوِيلاً فَقَامَ رَجُلٌ فَقَالَ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ زَوِّجْنِيهَا إِنْ لَمْ تَكُنْ لَكَ بِهَا حَاجَةٌ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ هَلْ عِنْدَكَ مِنْ شَىْءٍ تُصْدِقُهَا إِيَّاهُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ مَا عِنْدِي إِلاَّ إِزَارِي هَذَا ‏.‏ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ إِنْ أَعْطَيْتَهَا إِيَّاهُ جَلَسْتَ لاَ إِزَارَ لَكَ فَالْتَمِسْ شَيْئًا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ مَا أَجِدُ شَيْئًا ‏.‏ قَالَ ‏&quot;‏ الْتَمِسْ وَلَوْ خَاتَمًا مِنْ حَدِيدٍ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ فَالْتَمَسَ فَلَمْ يَجِدْ شَيْئًا ‏.‏ فَقَالَ لَهُ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ هَلْ مَعَكَ مِنَ الْقُرْآنِ شَىْءٌ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ نَعَمْ مَعِي سُورَةُ كَذَا وَسُورَةُ كَذَا ‏.‏ لِسُوَرٍ سَمَّاهَا ‏.‏ فَقَالَ لَهُ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ قَدْ أَنْكَحْتُكَهَا بِمَا مَعَكَ مِنَ الْقُرْآنِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1098]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1098" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Malik from Abu Hazim ibn Dinar from Sahl ibn Sad as-Saidi that a woman came to the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, and said, &quot;Messenger of Allah! I have given myself to you.&quot; She stood for a long time, and then a man got up and said, &quot;Messenger of Allah, marry her to me if you have no need of her.&quot; The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Do you have anything to give her as a bride-price?&quot; He said, &quot;I possess only this lower garment of mine.&quot; The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;If you give it to her you will not have a garment to wear so look for something else.&quot; He said, &quot;I have nothing else.&quot; He said, &quot;Look for something else, even if it is only an iron ring.&quot; He looked, and found that he had nothing. The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Do you know any of the Qur&#x27;an?&quot; He said, &quot;Yes. I know such-and-such a sura and such-and-such a sura,&quot; which he named. The Messengerof Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said to him, &quot;I have married her to you for what you know of the Qur&#x27;an</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1098]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1098" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">सहल बिन सअद सअदी से रिवायत है के एक औरत रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के पास आयी और कहा के मैं ने अपनी जान आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के लिए बख़श दी। वो देर तक खड़ी रही, फिर एक शख़्स उठा और बोला: या रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम)! अगर आपको इस से निकाह की कोई हाजत नहीं तो मेरा निकाह इस से कर दीजिये। आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने पूछा: क्या तेरे पास मेहर में देने के लिए कुछ है? उसने कहा: इस तहबंद के इलावा मेरे पास कुछ नहीं है। आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: अगर तू यह तहबंद दे देगा तो ख़ुद नंगा रहेगा, कोई और चीज़ ढूंढ ले। उसने कहा कुछ नहीं मिलता। आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: ढूंढो, अगरचे लोहे की एक अंगूठी ही क्यों न हो। उसने कोशिश की पर कुछ न मिला। फिर आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने पूछा: क्या तुझे क़ुरआन याद है? कहा: हां, फलां फलां सूरतें याद हैं। आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: मैंने इस क़ुरआन के इवज़ जो तुझे याद है, इस औरत का निकाह तेरे साथ कर दिया।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1098]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1098" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">سہل بن سعد ساعدی سے روایت ہے کہ ایک عورت جناب رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے پاس آئی اس نے کہا کہ تحقیق میں نے اپنی جان آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے واسطے بخشی اور کھڑی رہی دیر تک پھر ایک شخص کھڑا ہوا اور کہا یا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم اس سے میرا نکاح کر دو اگر اس سے نکاح کی آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کو کچھ حاجت نہیں ہے آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا تیرے پاس کوئی چیز ہے کہ مہر میں دے اس کو وہ شخص بولا سوائے اس تہبند کے میرے پاس کچھ نہیں ہے آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا اگر تو اپنا تہبند اس کو دے دے گا تو بغیر تہنبد کے بیٹھے گا کوئی چیز ڈھونڈ لے اس نے کہا مجھے کچھ نہیں ملتا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا ڈھونڈو اگرچہ لوہے کی ایک انگوٹھی ہو اس نے ڈھونڈا مگر کچھ نہ ملا تب آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے پوچھا تجھے قرآن یاد ہے بولا ہاں فلانی فلانی سورت یاد ہے کئی سورتوں کا نام لیا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا میں نے اس قرآن کے عوض میں اس عورت کا نکاح تیرے ساتھ کر دیا جو تجھ کو یاد ہے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1098]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1098" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Sahl bin Sa&#x27;ad Sa&#x27;idi se riwayat hai ke ek aurat Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke paas aayi aur kaha ke main ne apni jaan Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke liye bakhash di. Woh der tak khadi rahi, phir ek shakhs utha aur bola: Ya Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam)! Agar aapko is se nikah ki koi haajat nahi to mera nikah is se kar dijiye. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne pucha: Kya tere paas mehar mein dene ke liye kuch hai? Usne kaha: Is tehband ke ilawa mere paas kuch nahi hai. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Agar tu yeh tehband de dega to khud nanga rahega, koi aur cheez dhoondh le. Usne kaha kuch nahi milta. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Dhoondho, agarche lohe ki ek angoothi hi kyun na ho. Usne koshish ki par kuch na mila. Phir Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne pucha: Kya tujhe Quran yaad hai? Kaha: Haan, fulani fulani suratein yaad hain. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Maine is Quran ke ewaz jo tujhe yaad hai, is aurat ka nikah tere sath kar diya.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1098]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1098" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1089

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1099)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 28</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1099]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1099" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1099</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1099 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ الْمُسَيَّبِ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ قَالَ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ أَيُّمَا رَجُلٍ تَزَوَّجَ امْرَأَةً وَبِهَا جُنُونٌ أَوْ جُذَامٌ أَوْ بَرَصٌ فَمَسَّهَا فَلَهَا صَدَاقُهَا كَامِلاً وَذَلِكَ لِزَوْجِهَا غُرْمٌ عَلَى وَلِيِّهَا ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَإِنَّمَا يَكُونُ ذَلِكَ غُرْمًا عَلَى وَلِيِّهَا لِزَوْجِهَا إِذَا كَانَ وَلِيُّهَا الَّذِي أَنْكَحَهَا هُوَ أَبُوهَا أَوْ أَخُوهَا أَوْ مَنْ يُرَى أَنَّهُ يَعْلَمُ ذَلِكَ مِنْهَا فَأَمَّا إِذَا كَانَ وَلِيُّهَا الَّذِي أَنْكَحَهَا ابْنَ عَمٍّ أَوْ مَوْلًى أَوْ مِنَ الْعَشِيرَةِ مِمَّنْ يُرَى أَنَّهُ لاَ يَعْلَمُ ذَلِكَ مِنْهَا فَلَيْسَ عَلَيْهِ غُرْمٌ وَتَرُدُّ تِلْكَ الْمَرْأَةُ مَا أَخَذَتْهُ مِنْ صَدَاقِهَا وَيَتْرُكُ لَهَا قَدْرَ مَا تُسْتَحَلُّ بِهِ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1099" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Yahya ibn Said that Said ibn al-Musayyab had said that Umar ibn al-Khattab said, &quot;If a man marries a woman who is insane, or has leprosy or white leprosy, without being told of her condition by her guardian, and he has sexual relations with her, she keeps her bride-price in its entirety. Her husband has damages against her guardian.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1099]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1099" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1099</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1099 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;The husband has damages against her guardian when the guardian is her father, brother, or one who is deemed to have knowledge of her condition. If the guardian who gives her in marriage is a nephew, a mawla or a member of her tribe who is not deemed to have knowledge of her condition, there are no damages against him, and the woman returns what she has taken of her bride-price, and the husband leaves her whatever amount is thought to be fair</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1099" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1099]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1099" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1099 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">सईद बिन मुसय्यब ने कहा के हज़रत उमर बिन ख़त्ताब (रज़ि अल्लाहू अन्हु) ने फ़रमाया: जो शख़्स किसी औरत से निकाह करे और उसे जुनून, जुज़ाम या बरस (leprosy) की बीमारी हो और ख़ावंद न जानते हुए उस से जिमा कर ले, तो औरत को ख़ावंद पूरा मेहर दे और उसके वली से वापस ले ले। इमाम मालिक ने कहा: वली को मेहर इस सूरत में वापस देना होगा जब वो औरत का बाप या भाई हो जो उसका हाल जानता हो। और अगर वली महरम न हो (जैसे चाचा का बेटा) जिसे हाल मालूम न हो, तो उस पर मेहर फिरना लाज़िम नहीं, बल्कि औरत से मेहर वापस लिया जाएगा, सिर्फ़ इतना हिस्सा छोड़ दिया जाएगा जितने से उसकी फ़र्ज हलाल हुई।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1099" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">سعید بن مسیب نے کہا کہ حضرت عمر بن خطاب نے فرمایا کہ جو شخص کسی عورت سے نکاح کرے اور اس کو جنوں یا جذام یا برص ہو اور خاوند نہ جان کر اس سے جماع کرے اس عورت کو خاوند پورا مہرے دے اور اس کے ولی سے پھیر لے ۔ کہا مالک نے ولی کو مہر اس صورت میں واپس دینا ہوگا جب وہ عورت کا باپ یا بھائی یا ایسا قریب ہو کہ عورت کا حال جانتا ہو اور جو ولی محرم نہ ہو جیسے چچا کا بیٹا یا مولیٰ یا اور کوئی کنبے والا ہو جس کو عورت کا حال معلوم نہ ہو تو اس پر مہر پھیرنا لازم نہ ہوگا بلکہ اس عورت سے مہر پھیر لیا جائے گا صرف اس قدر چھوڑ دیا جائے گا جس سے اس کی فرج حلال ہو ۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1099" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1099" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1099 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Saeed bin Musayyib ne kaha ke Hazrat Umar bin Khattab (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne farmaya: Jo shakhs kisi aurat se nikah kare aur usay junoon, juzam ya baras (leprosy) ki bimari ho aur khawand na jante hue us se jima kar le, to aurat ko khawand pura mehar de aur uske wali se wapas le le. Imam Malik ne kaha: Wali ko mehar is surat mein wapas dena hoga jab woh aurat ka baap ya bhai ho jo uska haal janta ho. Aur agar wali mehram na ho (jaise chacha ka beta) jise haal maloom na ho, to us par mehar phirna lazim nahi, balki aurat se mehar wapas liya jayega, sirf itna hissa chhod diya jayega jitne se uski farj halal hui.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1099" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1090

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1100)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 28</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1100]</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1100</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1100 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، أَنَّ ابْنَةَ عُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، - وَأُمُّهَا بِنْتُ زَيْدِ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ - كَانَتْ تَحْتَ ابْنٍ لِعَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ فَمَاتَ وَلَمْ يَدْخُلْ بِهَا وَلَمْ يُسَمِّ لَهَا صَدَاقًا فَابْتَغَتْ أُمُّهَا صَدَاقَهَا فَقَالَ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ عُمَرَ لَيْسَ لَهَا صَدَاقٌ وَلَوْ كَانَ لَهَا صَدَاقٌ لَمْ نُمْسِكْهُ وَلَمْ نَظْلِمْهَا ‏.‏ فَأَبَتْ أُمُّهَا أَنْ تَقْبَلَ ذَلِكَ فَجَعَلُوا بَيْنَهُمْ زَيْدَ بْنَ ثَابِتٍ فَقَضَى أَنْ لاَ صَدَاقَ لَهَا وَلَهَا الْمِيرَاثُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1100" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Umar (companion); Zayd ibn Thabit (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi that the daughter of Ubaydullah ibn Umar whose mother was the daughter of Zayd ibn al- Khattab, married the son of Abdullah ibn Umar. He died and had not yet consummated the marriage or specified her bride-price. Her mother wanted the bride-price, and Abdullah ibn Umar said, &quot;She is not entitled to a bride-price. Had she been entitled to a bride-price, we would not have kept it and we would not do her an injustice. &quot;The mother refused to accept that. Zayd ibn Thabit was brought to adjudicate between them and he decided that she had no bride-price, but that she did inherit</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1100]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1100" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">यहया ने मुझ से इमाम मालिक से रिवायत किया के उबैदुल्लाह बिन उमर की बेटी, जिस की मां ज़ैद बिन ख़त्ताब की बेटी थी, ने अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर के बेटे से निकाह किया। उस का इंतेक़ाल हो गया और अभी रुख़सती नहीं हुई थी और न ही मेहर मुक़र्रर हुआ था। उस की मां ने मेहर मांगा तो अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर ने कहा के वो मेहर की हक़दार नहीं है। अगर वो हक़दार होती तो हम उसे न रोकते और न उस पर ज़ुल्म करते। मां ने इंकार किया, तब ज़ैद बिन साबित ने फ़ैसला किया के उसे मेहर नहीं मिलेगा मगर वो मीरास पाएगी।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1100" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">یحییٰ نے مجھ سے امام مالک سے روایت کیا کہ عبیداللہ بن عمر کی بیٹی، جس کی ماں زید بن خطاب کی بیٹی تھی، نے عبداللہ بن عمر کے بیٹے سے نکاح کیا۔ اس کا انتقال ہو گیا اور ابھی رخصتی نہیں ہوئی تھی اور نہ ہی مہر مقرر ہوا تھا۔ اس کی ماں نے مہر مانگا تو عبداللہ بن عمر نے کہا کہ وہ مہر کی حقدار نہیں ہے۔ اگر وہ حقدار ہوتی تو ہم اسے نہ روکتے اور نہ اس پر ظلم کرتے۔ ماں نے انکار کیا، تب زید بن ثابت نے فیصلہ کیا کہ اسے مہر نہیں ملے گا مگر وہ میراث پائے گی۔</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1100 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya ne mujh se Imam Malik se riwayat kiya ke Ubaidullah bin Umar ki beti, jis ki maa Zaid bin Khattab ki beti thi, ne Abdullah bin Umar ke bete se nikah kiya. Us ka inteqal ho gaya aur abhi rukhsati nahi hui thi aur na hi mehar muqarrar hua tha. Us ki maa ne mehar manga to Abdullah bin Umar ne kaha ke wo mehar ki haqdar nahi hai. Agar wo haqdar hoti to hum use na rokte aur na us par zulm karte. Maa ne inkar kiya, tab Zaid bin Thabit ne faisla kiya ke use mehar nahi milega magar wo meeras payegi.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1100" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1091

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1101)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 28</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1101]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1101" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1101</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1101 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ عُمَرَ بْنَ عَبْدِ الْعَزِيزِ، كَتَبَ فِي خِلاَفَتِهِ إِلَى بَعْضِ عُمَّالِهِ أَنَّ كُلَّ مَا اشْتَرَطَ الْمُنْكِحُ - مَنْ كَانَ أَبًا أَوْ غَيْرَهُ - مِنْ حِبَاءٍ أَوْ كَرَامَةٍ فَهُوَ لِلْمَرْأَةِ إِنِ ابْتَغَتْهُ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فِي الْمَرْأَةِ يُنْكِحُهَا أَبُوهَا وَيَشْتَرِطُ فِي صَدَاقِهَا الْحِبَاءَ يُحْبَى بِهِ إِنَّ مَا كَانَ مِنْ شَرْطٍ يَقَعُ بِهِ النِّكَاحُ فَهُوَ لاِبْنَتِهِ إِنِ ابْتَغَتْهُ وَإِنْ فَارَقَهَا زَوْجُهَا قَبْلَ أَنْ يَدْخُلَ بِهَا فَلِزَوْجِهَا شَطْرُ الْحِبَاءِ الَّذِي وَقَعَ بِهِ النِّكَاحُ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فِي الرَّجُلِ يُزَوِّجُ ابْنَهُ صَغِيرًا لاَ مَالَ لَهُ إِنَّ الصَّدَاقَ عَلَى أَبِيهِ إِذَا كَانَ الْغُلاَمُ يَوْمَ تَزَوَّجَ لاَ مَالَ لَهُ وَإِنْ كَانَ لِلْغُلاَمِ مَالٌ فَالصَّدَاقُ فِي مَالِ الْغُلاَمِ إِلاَّ أَنْ يُسَمِّيَ الأَبُ أَنَّ الصَّدَاقَ عَلَيْهِ وَذَلِكَ النِّكَاحُ ثَابِتٌ عَلَى الاِبْنِ إِذَا كَانَ صَغِيرًا وَكَانَ فِي وِلاَيَةِ أَبِيهِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فِي طَلاَقِ الرَّجُلِ امْرَأَتَهُ قَبْلَ أَنْ يَدْخُلَ بِهَا وَهِيَ بِكْرٌ فَيَعْفُوَ أَبُوهَا عَنْ نِصْفِ الصَّدَاقِ إِنَّ ذَلِكَ جَائِزٌ لِزَوْجِهَا مِنْ أَبِيهَا فِيمَا وَضَعَ عَنْهُ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَذَلِكَ أَنَّ اللَّهَ تَبَارَكَ وَتَعَالَى قَالَ فِي كِتَابِهِ ‏{‏إِلاَّ أَنْ يَعْفُونَ‏}‏ فَهُنَّ النِّسَاءُ اللاَّتِي قَدْ دُخِلَ بِهِنَّ ‏{‏أَوْ يَعْفُوَ الَّذِي بِيَدِهِ عُقْدَةُ النِّكَاحِ‏}‏ فَهُوَ الأَبُ فِي ابْنَتِهِ الْبِكْرِ وَالسَّيِّدُ فِي أَمَتِهِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَهَذَا الَّذِي سَمِعْتُ فِي ذَلِكَ وَالَّذِي عَلَيْهِ الأَمْرُ عِنْدَنَا ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فِي الْيَهُودِيَّةِ أَوِ النَّصْرَانِيَّةِ تَحْتَ الْيَهُودِيِّ أَوِ النَّصْرَانِيِّ فَتُسْلِمُ قَبْلَ أَنْ يَدْخُلَ بِهَا أَنَّهُ لاَ صَدَاقَ لَهَا ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ لاَ أَرَى أَنْ تُنْكَحَ الْمَرْأَةُ بِأَقَلَّ مِنْ رُبْعِ دِينَارٍ وَذَلِكَ أَدْنَى مَا يَجِبُ فِيهِ الْقَطْعُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1101" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: historical report. Attribution: Umar ibn Abd al-Aziz (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that Umar ibn Abd al-Aziz during his khalifate, wrote to one of his governors, &quot;Whatever a father, or guardian, who gives someone in marriage, makes a condition in the way of unreturnable gift or of favour, belongs to the woman if she wants it.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1101]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1101" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1101</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1101 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik spoke about a woman whose father gave her in marriage and made an unreturnable gift a condition of the bride-price which was to be given. He said, &quot;Whatever is given as a condition by which marriage occurs belongs to the woman if she wants it. If the husband parts from her before the marriage is consummated, the husband has half of the unreturnable gift by which the marriage occurred.&quot; Malik said about a man who married off his young son and the son had no wealth at all, that the bride- price was obliged of the father if the young man had no property on the day of marriage. If the young man did have property the bride- price was taken from his property unless the father stipulated that he would pay the bride-price. The marriage was affirmed for the son if he was a minor only if he was under the guardianship of his father. Malik said that if a man divorced his wife before he had consummated the marriage and she was a virgin, her father returned half of the bride-price to him. That half was permitted to the husband from the father to compensate him for his expenses. Malik said that that was because Allah, the Blessed, the Exalted, said in His Book, &quot;Unless they (women with whom he had not consummated marriage) make remission or he makes remission to him in whose hand is the knot of marriage.&quot; (Sura 2 ayat 237). (He being the father of a virgin daughter or the master of a female slave.) Malik said, &quot;That is what I have heard about the matter, and that is how things are done among us.&quot; Malik said that a jewish or christian woman who was married to a jew or christian and then became muslim before the marriage had been consummated, did not keep anything from the bride-price. Malik said, &quot;I do not think that women should be married for less than a quarter of a dinar. That is the lowest amount for which cutting off the hand is obliged</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1101" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1101]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1101" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1101</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1101 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">नाफ़े से रिवायत है के उबैदुल्लाह बिन उमर की बेटी जिन की मां ज़ैद बिन ख़त्ताब की बेटी थीं अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर के बेटे के निकाह में आयी, वो मर गए मगर उन्होंने उस से सोहबत नहीं की न उन का मेहर मुक़र्रर हुआ था। तो उन की मां ने मेहर मांगा, अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर ने कहा के मेहर का उन को इस्तेहक़ाक़ नहीं अगर होता तो हम रख न लेते न ज़ुल्म करते, उन की मां ने न माना। ज़ैद बिन साबित के कहने पर रखा, ज़ैद ने ये फ़ैसला किया के उन को मेहर नहीं मिलेगा अलबत्ता तरका मिलेगा। उमर बिन अब्दुल अज़ीज़ ने अपने आमिल को लिखा के निकाह कर देने वाला बाप हो या कोई और अगर ख़ावंद से कुछ तोहफ़ा या हदिया लेने की शर्त करे तो वो औरत को मिलेगा अगर तलब करे। कहा मालिक ने जिस औरत का निकाह बाप कर दे और उस के मेहर में कुछ हिबा की शर्त करे अगर वो शर्त ऐसी हो जिस के इवज़ में निकाह हुआ है तो वो हिबा उस की बेटी को मिलेगा अगर चाहे। कहा मालिक ने जो शख़्स अपनी नाबालिग लड़की का निकाह करे और उस लड़के का कोई ज़ाती माल न हो तो मेहर उस के बाप पर वाजिब होगा और अगर उस लड़के का ज़ाती माल हो तो उस के माल में से दिलाया जाएगा मगर जिस सूरत में बाप मेहर को अपने ज़िम्मे कर ले और ये निकाह लड़की पर लाज़िम होगा जब वो नाबालिग हो और अपने बाप की विलायत में हो। कहा मालिक ने मेरे नज़दीक रबा दीनार से कम मेहर नहीं हो सकता और न रबा दीनार की चोरी में हाथ काटा जाएगा।</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">نافع سے روایت ہے کہ عبیداللہ بن عمر کی بیٹی جن کی ماں زید بن خطاب کی بیٹی تھیں عبداللہ بن عمر کے بیٹے کے نکاح میں آئی وہ مر گئے مگر انہوں نے اس سے صحبت نہیں کی نہ ان کا مہر مقرر ہوا تھا تو ان کی ماں نے مہر مانگا عبداللہ بن عمر نے کہا کہ مہر کا ان کو استحقاق نہیں اگر ہوتا تو ہم رکھ نہ لیتے نہ ظلم کرتے ان کی ماں نے نہ مانا زید بن ثابت کے کہنے پر رکھا زید نے یہ فیصلہ کیا کہ ان کو مہر نہیں ملے گا البتہ ترکہ ملے گا ۔ عمر بن عبدالعزیز نے اپنے عامل کو لکھا کہ نکاح کر دینے والا باپ ہو یا کوئی اور اگر خاوند سے کچھ تحفہ یا ہدیہ لینے کی شرط کرے تو وہ عورت کو ملے گا اگر طلب کرے ۔ کہا مالک نے جس عورت کا نکاح باپ کر دے اور اس کے مہر میں کچھ حبا کی شرط کرے اگر وہ شرط ایسی ہو جس کے عوض میں نکاح ہوا ہے تو وہ حبا اس کی بیٹی کو ملے گا اگر چاہے ۔ کہا مالک نے جو شخص اپنی نا بالغ لڑکی کا نکاح کرے اور اس لڑکے کا کوئی ذاتی مال نہ ہو تو مہراس کے باپ پر واجب ہوگا اور اگر اس لڑکے کا ذاتی مال ہو تو اس کے مال میں سے دلایا جائے گا مگر جس صورت میں باپ مہر کو اپنے ذمے کر لے اور یہ نکاح لڑکی پر لازم ہوگا جب وہ نابالغ ہو اور اپنے باپ کی ولایت میں ہو ۔ کہا مالک نے میرے نزدیک ربع دینار سے کم مہر نہیں ہو سکتا اور نہ ربع دینار کی چوری میں ہاتھ کاٹا جائے گا ۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1101" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1101 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Nafi se riwayat hai ke Ubaidullah bin Umar ki beti jin ki maa Zaid bin Khattab ki beti thin Abdullah bin Umar ke bete ke nikah mein aayi, wo mar gaye magar unhon ne us se sohbat nahi ki na un ka mehar muqarrar hua tha. To un ki maa ne mehar manga, Abdullah bin Umar ne kaha ke mehar ka un ko istehqaq nahi agar hota to hum rakh na lete na zulm karte, un ki maa ne na mana. Zaid bin Thabit ke kehne par rakha, Zaid ne ye faisla kiya ke un ko mehar nahi milega albata tarka milega. Umar bin Abdul Aziz ne apne aamil ko likha ke nikah kar dene wala baap ho ya koi aur agar khawand se kuch tohfa ya hadiya lene ki shart kare to wo aurat ko milega agar talab kare. Kaha Malik ne jis aurat ka nikah baap kar de aur us ke mehar mein kuch hiba ki shart kare agar wo shart aisi ho jis ke ewaz mein nikah hua hai to wo hiba us ki beti ko milega agar chahe. Kaha Malik ne jo shakhs apni nabaligh ladki ka nikah kare aur us ladke ka koi zati maal na ho to mehar us ke baap par wajib hoga aur agar us ladke ka zati maal ho to us ke maal mein se dilaya jayega magar jis surat mein baap mehar ko apne zimme kar le aur ye nikah ladki par lazim hoga jab wo nabaligh ho aur apne baap ki wilayat mein ho. Kaha Malik ne mere nazdeek rab&#x27;a dinar se kam mehar nahi ho sakta aur na rab&#x27;a dinar ki chori mein hath kaata jayega.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1101" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1092

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>
