<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ الْمُسَيَّبِ، أَنَّ عُمَرَ بْنَ الْخَطَّابِ، قَضَى فِي الْمَرْأَةِ إِذَا تَزَوَّجَهَا الرَّجُلُ أَنَّهُ إِذَا أُرْخِيَتِ السُّتُورُ فَقَدْ وَجَبَ الصَّدَاقُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1102" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Yahya ibn Said from Said ibn al-Musayyab that &#x27;Umar ibn al-Khattab decided about the woman who was married by a man and the marriage had been consummated, that the bride-price was obligatory</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1102]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Zayd ibn Thabit (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab that Zayd ibn Thabit said, &quot;When a man takes his wife to his house and co-habits with her then the bride-price is obliged</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1103]</p>

## Narration 1094

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1104)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 28</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1104]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1104" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1104</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1104 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ سَعِيدَ بْنَ الْمُسَيَّبِ، كَانَ يَقُولُ إِذَا دَخَلَ الرَّجُلُ بِالْمَرْأَةِ فِي بَيْتِهَا صُدِّقَ الرَّجُلُ عَلَيْهَا وَإِذَا دَخَلَتْ عَلَيْهِ فِي بَيْتِهِ صُدِّقَتْ عَلَيْهِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ أَرَى ذَلِكَ فِي الْمَسِيسِ إِذَا دَخَلَ عَلَيْهَا فِي بَيْتِهَا فَقَالَتْ قَدْ مَسَّنِي وَقَالَ لَمْ أَمَسَّهَا صُدِّقَ عَلَيْهَا فَإِنْ دَخَلَتْ عَلَيْهِ فِي بَيْتِهِ فَقَالَ لَمْ أَمَسَّهَا وَقَالَتْ قَدْ مَسَّنِي صُدِّقَتْ عَلَيْهِ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1104" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Said ibn al-Musayyab (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that Said ibn al-Musayyab said, &quot;When a man comes to his wife in her room, he is believed. When she comes to him in his room, she is believed.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1104]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1104" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1104</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1104 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik commented, &quot;I think that this refers to sexual intercourse. When he comes in to her in her room and she says, &#x27;He has had intercourse with me&#x27; and he says, &#x27;I have not touched her&#x27;, he is believed. When she comes in to him in his room and he says, &#x27;I have not had intercourse with her&#x27; and she says, &#x27;He had intercourse with me&#x27;, she is believed</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1104" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

## Narration 1095

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1105)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 28</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ أَبِي بَكْرِ بْنِ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ عَمْرِو بْنِ حَزْمٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الْمَلِكِ بْنِ أَبِي بَكْرِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ الْحَارِثِ بْنِ هِشَامٍ الْمَخْزُومِيِّ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم حِينَ تَزَوَّجَ أُمَّ سَلَمَةَ وَأَصْبَحَتْ عِنْدَهُ قَالَ لَهَا ‏ &quot;‏ لَيْسَ بِكِ عَلَى أَهْلِكِ هَوَانٌ إِنْ شِئْتِ سَبَّعْتُ عِنْدَكِ وَسَبَّعْتُ عِنْدَهُنَّ وَإِنْ شِئْتِ ثَلَّثْتُ عِنْدَكِ وَدُرْتُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ فَقَالَتْ ثَلِّثْ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1105]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1105" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abdullah ibn Abi Bakr ibn Muhammad ibn Amr ibn Hazm from Abd al-Malik ibn Abi Bakr ibn Abd ar- Rahman ibn al-Harith ibn Hisham al-Makhzumi from his father that when the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, married Umm Salama and then spent the night with her, he said to her, &quot;You are not being humbled in your right. If you wish, I will stay with you for seven nights as I stayed seven nights with the others. If you wish, I will stay with you for three nights, and then visit the others in turn.&quot; She said, &quot;Stay three nights</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1105]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1105" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">यह्या ने मुझ से इमाम मालिक से रिवायत की, उन्होंने अब्दुल्लाह बिन अबी बक्र बिन मुहम्मद बिन अम्र बिन हज़म से, उन्होंने अपने वालिद से बयान किया के जब रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने उम्म सलमा से निकाह किया और उनके साथ रात गुज़ारी तोह आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: तुम्हारे हक़ में कोई कमी नहीं की जाएगी। अगर तुम चाहो तोह मैं तुम्हारे पास सात रातें रहूँगा जैसे मैंने दूसरों के पास सात रातें गुज़ारें, और अगर तुम चाहो तोह मैं तुम्हारे पास तीन रातें रहूँगा फिर बारी बारी दूसरों के पास जाऊँगा। उन्होंने कहा: आप तीन रातें रहें।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1105]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1105" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">یحییٰ نے مجھ سے امام مالک سے روایت کی، انہوں نے عبداللہ بن ابی بکر بن محمد بن عمرو بن حزم سے، انہوں نے اپنے والد سے بیان کیا کہ جب رسول اللہ (صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم) نے ام سلمہ سے نکاح کیا اور ان کے ساتھ رات گزاری تو آپ (صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم) نے فرمایا: تمہارے حق میں کوئی کمی نہیں کی جائے گی۔ اگر تم چاہو تو میں تمہارے پاس سات راتیں رہوں گا جیسے میں نے دوسروں کے پاس سات راتیں گزاریں، اور اگر تم چاہو تو میں تمہارے پاس تین راتیں رہوں گا پھر باری باری دوسروں کے پاس جاؤں گا۔ انہوں نے کہا: آپ تین راتیں رہیں۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1105]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1105" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya ne mujh se Imam Malik se riwayat ki, unhone Abdullah bin Abi Bakr bin Muhammad bin Amr bin Hazm se, unhone apne walid se bayan kiya ke jab Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne Umm Salama se nikah kiya aur unke sath raat guzari toh Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Tumhare haq mein koi kami nahi ki jayegi. Agar tum chaho toh main tumhare paas saat ratein rahoonga jaise maine dusron ke paas saat ratein guzarein, aur agar tum chaho toh main tumhare paas teen ratein rahoonga phir bari bari dusron ke paas jaoonga. Unhone kaha: Aap teen ratein rahein.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1105]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Anas ibn Malik (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Humayd at-Tawil that Anas ibn Malik said, &quot;A virgin has seven nights, and a woman who has been previously married has three nights.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1106]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1106" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1106 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik affirmed, &quot;That is what is done among us.&quot; Malik added, &quot;If the man has another wife, he divides his time equally between them after the wedding nights. He does not count the wedding nights against the one he has just married</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1106" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ سَعِيدَ بْنَ الْمُسَيَّبِ، سُئِلَ عَنِ الْمَرْأَةِ، تَشْتَرِطُ عَلَى زَوْجِهَا أَنَّهُ لاَ يَخْرُجُ بِهَا مِنْ بَلَدِهَا فَقَالَ سَعِيدُ بْنُ الْمُسَيَّبِ يَخْرُجُ بِهَا إِنْ شَاءَ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فَالأَمْرُ عِنْدَنَا ذَلِكَ أَنَّهُ إِذَا شَرَطَ الرَّجُلُ لِلْمَرْأَةِ وَإِنْ كَانَ ذَلِكَ عِنْدَ عُقْدَةِ النِّكَاحِ أَنْ لاَ أَنْكِحَ عَلَيْكِ وَلاَ أَتَسَرَّرَ إِنَّ ذَلِكَ لَيْسَ بِشَىْءٍ إِلاَّ أَنْ يَكُونَ فِي ذَلِكَ يَمِينٌ بِطَلاَقٍ أَوْ عِتَاقَةٍ فَيَجِبُ ذَلِكَ عَلَيْهِ وَيَلْزَمُهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1107" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Said ibn al-Musayyab (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that Said ibn al-Musayyab was asked about a woman who made a stipulation on her husband not to take her away from her town. Said ibn al-Musayyab said, &quot;He takes her away if he wishes.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1107]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1107" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1107</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1107 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;The custom among us is that when a man marries a woman, and he makes a condition in the marriage contract that he will not marry after her or take a concubine, it means nothing unless there is an oath of divorce or setting-free attached to it. Then it is obliged and required of him</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1107" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1098

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1108)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 28</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1108]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1108" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1108</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1108 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ الْمِسْوَرِ بْنِ رِفَاعَةَ الْقُرَظِيِّ، عَنِ الزُّبَيْرِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ الزَّبِيرِ، أَنَّ رِفَاعَةَ بْنَ سِمْوَالٍ، طَلَّقَ امْرَأَتَهُ تَمِيمَةَ بِنْتَ وَهْبٍ فِي عَهْدِ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ثَلاَثًا فَنَكَحَتْ عَبْدَ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنَ الزَّبِيرِ فَاعْتَرَضَ عَنْهَا فَلَمْ يَسْتَطِعْ أَنْ يَمَسَّهَا فَفَارَقَهَا فَأَرَادَ رِفَاعَةُ أَنْ يَنْكِحَهَا - وَهُوَ زَوْجُهَا الأَوَّلُ الَّذِي كَانَ طَلَّقَهَا - فَذَكَرَ ذَلِكَ لِرَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَنَهَاهُ عَنْ تَزْوِيجِهَا وَقَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ لاَ تَحِلُّ لَكَ حَتَّى تَذُوقَ الْعُسَيْلَةَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1108" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: Muhammad, as explicitly attributed in this complete source field. Exact English field independently read in full. This locally supported Prophetic report is preserved as its own exact source passage; companion/jurist discussion outside the selected passage remains source context. No authenticity or translation-equivalence judgment is made.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from al-Miswar ibn Rifaa al- Quradhi from az-Zubayr ibn Abd ar-Rahman ibn az-Zubayr that Rifaa ibn Simwal divorced his wife, Tamima bint Wahb, in the time of the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, three times. Then she married Abd ar-Rahman ibn az-Zubayr and he turned from her and could not consummate the marriage and so he parted from her. Rifaa wanted to marry her again and it was mentioned to the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, and he forbade him to marry her. He said, &quot;She is not halal for you until she has tasted the sweetness of intercourse</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1108]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1108" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">ज़ुबैर बिन अब्दुर रहमान (रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रिफ़ाआ बिन समवल क़ुरज़ी ने अपनी बीवी तमीमा बिन्त वहब को रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के ज़माने में तीन तलाक़ें दीं तो उनहों ने निकाह किया अब्दुर रहमान बिन ज़ुबैर से मगर अब्दुर रहमान उस पर क़ादिर न हुए और जमा न कर सके, इस वास्ते अब्दुर रहमान ने उस को छोड़ दिया। तब रिफ़ाआ जो शौहर-ए-अव्वल थे उनहों ने फिर निकाह करना चाहा, जब आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से इस का ज़िक्र हुआ आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने मना किया और रिफ़ाआ से फ़रमाया के वो औरत तुझ को हलाल नहीं जब तक दूसरे शख़्स से जमा न कराए।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1108" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">زبیر بن عبدالرحمن رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ روایت ہے کہ رفاعہ بن سموال قرظی نے اپنی بی بی تمیمہ بنت وہب کو رسول اللہ کے زمانے میں تین طلاقیں دیں تو انہوں نے نکاح کیا عبدالرحمن بن زبیر سے مگر عبدالرحمن اس پر قادر نہ ہوئے اور جماع نہ کر سکے اس واسطے عبدالرحمن نے اس کو چھوڑ دیا تب رفاعہ جو شوہر اول تھے انہوں نے پھر نکاح کرنا چاہا جب آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے اس کا ذکر ہوا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے منع کیا اور رفاعہ سے فرمایا کہ وہ عورت تجھ کو حلال نہیں جب تک دوسرے شخص سے جماع نہ کرائے ۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1108" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1108]</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Zubair bin Abdur Rahman (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Rifa&#x27;a bin Samwal Qurzi ne apni biwi Tamima bint Wahb ko Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke zamane mein teen talaqein dein to unhon ne nikah kiya Abdur Rahman bin Zubair se magar Abdur Rahman us par qadir na hue aur jama na kar sake, is waste Abdur Rahman ne us ko chhod diya. Tab Rifa&#x27;a jo shohar-e-awwal the unhon ne phir nikah karna chaha, jab Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se is ka zikr hua Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne mana kiya aur Rifa&#x27;a se farmaya ke wo aurat tujh ko halal nahi jab tak dusre shakhs se jama na karaye.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1108" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1099

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 28</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنِ الْقَاسِمِ بْنِ مُحَمَّدٍ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ، زَوْجِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَنَّهَا سُئِلَتْ عَنْ رَجُلٍ طَلَّقَ امْرَأَتَهُ الْبَتَّةَ فَتَزَوَّجَهَا بَعْدَهُ رَجُلٌ آخَرُ فَطَلَّقَهَا قَبْلَ أَنْ يَمَسَّهَا هَلْ يَصْلُحُ لِزَوْجِهَا الأَوَّلِ أَنْ يَتَزَوَّجَهَا فَقَالَتْ عَائِشَةُ لاَ حَتَّى يَذُوقَ عُسَيْلَتَهَا ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1109" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: A&#x27;isha, the wife of the Prophet (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Yahya ibn Said from al-Qasim ibn Muhammad that A&#x27;isha, the wife of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said when asked whether it was permissible for a man to marry again a wife he had divorced irrevocably if she had married another man who divorced her before consummating the marriage, &quot;Not until she has tasted the sweetness of intercourse</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1109]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1109" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">हज़रात ऐषा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) से सवाल हुआ के एक शख्स अपनी औरत को जमा करने से पहले तीन तलाकें दे दे पहला शोहर इस से निकाह कर सकता है? जवाब दिया के नहीं कर सकता जब तक दूसरा शौहर इस से जमा न करे. क़ासिम बिन मुहम्मद से सवाल हुआ के एक शख्स ने अपनी औरत को तीन तलाकें दें फिर इस से दूसरे शख्स ने निकाह किया और वो जमा करने से पहले मर गया</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1109" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="rtl">حضرت عائشہ سے سوال ہوا کہ ایک شخص اپنی عورت کو جماع کرنے سے پہلے تین طلاقیں دیدے اب پہلا شوہر اس سے نکاح کر سکتا ہے جواب دیا کہ نہیں کر سکتا جب تک دوسرا شوہر اس سے جماع نہ کرے ۔ قاسم بن محمد سے سوال ہوا کہ ایک شخص نے اپنی عورت کو تین طلاقیں دیں پھر اس سے دوسرے شخص نے نکاح کیا اور وہ جماع کرنے سے پہلے مر گیا کیا پہلے شوہر کو اس سے نکاح کر لینا درست ہے جواب دیا نہیں ۔</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Hazrat Aisha (Radi Allahu Anha) se sawal hua ke ek shakhs apni aurat ko jama karne se pehle teen talaqein de de, ab pehla shohar is se nikah kar sakta hai? Jawab diya ke nahi kar sakta jab tak dusra shohar is se jama na kare. Qasim bin Muhammad se sawal hua ke ek shakhs ne apni aurat ko teen talaqein dein phir is se dusre shakhs ne nikah kiya aur wo jama karne se pehle mar gaya, kya pehle shohar ko is se nikah kar lena durust hai? Jawab diya nahi.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1109" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1100

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1110)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 28</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1110</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1110 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ الْقَاسِمَ بْنَ مُحَمَّدٍ، سُئِلَ عَنْ رَجُلٍ، طَلَّقَ امْرَأَتَهُ الْبَتَّةَ ثُمَّ تَزَوَّجَهَا بَعْدَهُ رَجُلٌ آخَرُ فَمَاتَ عَنْهَا قَبْلَ أَنْ يَمَسَّهَا هَلْ يَحِلُّ لِزَوْجِهَا الأَوَّلِ أَنْ يُرَاجِعَهَا فَقَالَ الْقَاسِمُ بْنُ مُحَمَّدٍ لاَ يَحِلُّ لِزَوْجِهَا الأَوَّلِ أَنْ يُرَاجِعَهَا ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فِي الْمُحَلِّلِ إِنَّهُ لاَ يُقِيمُ عَلَى نِكَاحِهِ ذَلِكَ حَتَّى يَسْتَقْبِلَ نِكَاحًا جَدِيدًا فَإِنْ أَصَابَهَا فِي ذَلِكَ فَلَهَا مَهْرُهَا ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1110" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: al-Qasim ibn Muhammad (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that when asked whether it was permissible for a man to return to his wife if he had divorced her irrevocably and then another man had married her after him and died before consummating the marriage, al-Qasim ibn Muhammad said, &quot;It is not halal for the first husband to return to her.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1110]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1110" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1110</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1110 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, about the muhallil, that he could not remain in the marriage until he undertook a new marriage. If he had intercourse with her in that marriage, she had her dowry</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1110" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">याह्या ने मुझ से इमाम मालिक से रिवायत की कि उन्होंने सुना जब कासिम बिन मुहम्मद से पूछा गया कि क्या किसी आदमी के लिए हलाल है के वह अपनी बीवी की तरफ़ रुजू करे अगर उसने उसे तीन तलाकें दे दी हों और उसके बाद किसी दूसरे आदमी ने उस से निकाह किया और हमबिस्तरी से पहले ही वह मर गया हलाल नहीं है. मलिक ने मुहल्लिल के बारे में कहा कि वह निकाह में तब तक नहीं रह सकता जब तक वह नया निकाह न करे. अगर उसने उस निकाह में उसके साथ हमबिस्तरी कर ली तोह उसका मेहर उसका हक़ है.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1110" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">یحییٰ نے مجھ سے امام مالک سے روایت کی کہ انہوں نے سنا جب قاسم بن محمد سے پوچھا گیا کہ کیا کسی آدمی کے لیے حلال ہے کہ وہ اپنی بیوی کی طرف رجوع کرے اگر اس نے اسے تین طلاقیں دے دی ہوں اور اس کے بعد کسی دوسرے آدمی نے اس سے نکاح کیا اور ہمبستری سے پہلے ہی وہ مر گیا، تو قاسم نے کہا: پہلے شوہر کے لیے اس کی طرف رجوع کرنا حلال نہیں ہے۔ مالک نے محلل کے بارے میں کہا کہ وہ نکاح میں تب تک نہیں رہ سکتا جب تک وہ نیا نکاح نہ کرے۔ اگر اس نے اس نکاح میں اس کے ساتھ ہمبستری کر لی تو اس کا مہر اس کا حق ہے۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1110" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya ne mujh se Imam Malik se riwayat ki ke unhone suna jab Qasim bin Muhammad se pucha gaya ke kya kisi aadmi ke liye halal hai ke woh apni biwi ki taraf ruju kare agar usne use teen talaqein de di hon aur uske baad kisi dusre aadmi ne us se nikah kiya aur hambistari se pehle hi woh mar gaya, toh Qasim ne kaha: Pehle shohar ke liye uski taraf ruju karna halal nahi hai. Malik ne muhallil ke bare mein kaha ke woh nikah mein tab tak nahi reh sakta jab tak woh naya nikah na kare. Agar usne us nikah mein uske sath hambistari kar li toh uska mehar uska haq hai.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1110" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>
